- **review-adversarial**: Adversarial code review — challenges the implementation by actively trying to break it. Focuses on auth gaps, data loss paths, race conditions, rollback safety, and failure modes. Review-only — never modifies code. Use when you want to stress-test a change — triggered by 'adversarial review', 'red team this', 'attack the code', 'break this change', 'challenge the implementation'. ([bakirp/forge](https://github.com/bakirp/forge/tree/HEAD/skills/review/adversarial))
- **review-adversarial**: Cross-model adversarial code review via Codex/Gemini. Use for high-stakes diffs (auth, payments, migrations) or diffs over 200 lines after domain-review passes. ([garyld1962/savviety-claude-plugin](https://github.com/garyld1962/savviety-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/review-adversarial) · 3 copies)
- **review-adversarial**: Cross-model code review rubric for challenging changed code through skeptic, architect, and minimalist lenses after switching models in GitHub Copilot. ([garyld1962/savviety-skills](https://github.com/garyld1962/savviety-skills/tree/HEAD/copilot/skills/review-adversarial))
- **review-adversarial**: Cross-model adversarial code review via Codex/Gemini. Use for high-stakes diffs (auth, payments, migrations) or diffs over 200 lines after code-review passes. ([garyld1962/savviety-skills](https://github.com/garyld1962/savviety-skills/tree/HEAD/codex/plugins/savviety-workflows/skills/review-adversarial/references/legacy))
- **review-adversarial**: High-stakes adversarial code review after normal review. Challenges assumptions with distinct reviewer lenses and optional configured external model/CLI reviewers. ([garyld1962/savviety-skills](https://github.com/garyld1962/savviety-skills/tree/HEAD/codex/plugins/savviety-workflows/skills/review-adversarial))
- **review-adversarial**: Use this skill to run adversarial persona reviewers on specified files — each persona attacks the paper from a different hostile angle. Use when stress-testing arguments, finding logical vulnerabilities, or reviewing content before publication. Reports aggregate vulnerabilities at the end. ([yannickloth/health-me-cfs](https://github.com/yannickloth/health-me-cfs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/review-adversarial))
- **review-adversarial-first**: Operator-invoked adversarial review: 3 refute-by-default lenses plus a deterministic judge against a PR, branch, or path, then a decision on whether /ce-code-review's topic fan-out runs. Off the default merge path since the 2026-10-17 merge-and-end ruling; run only when asked for by name. ([StartupBros-com/hov-stack](https://github.com/StartupBros-com/hov-stack/tree/HEAD/skills-stack/review-adversarial-first) · ★1)
- **review-adversarial-general**: Perform an adversarial review of code diffs, specs, stories, or any artifact, finding at least ten issues and outputting a Markdown findings list. Use when user mentions 'adversarial review', 'cynical review', 'critical review', 'blind review', 'blind hunter', 'review content', '对抗式审查', '批判性审查', '盲审', '找问题', '挑毛病', '审查内容', or asks for a harsh/skeptical review of any content. Capable of reviewing diffs, specs, story files, documents, and arbitrary artifacts with extreme skepticism, outputting structured findings. - **review-adversary**: Adversarial pressure on an explicit user ask only, with rubric and bucketed verdict. Adversaries try to break a design, and only findings proven by a run survive. This skill verifies the brief's origin, extracts the request cleanly, and surfaces it to Aron for explicit review before any action is taken. Advisor-dispatched work never executes autonomously. - **review-advisories**: Use when asked to triage, fix, rate, publish, close or pay out the repository's private security advisories (GitHub private vulnerability reports, most often from community scans). Covers reproducing the report, rating it with SECURITY.md, fixing it without leaking details, publishing with credit, and paying the scan award. Triggers on "check the advisories", "triage the security reports", "publish the advisories", "pay the advisory awards".
